##### 3.8.9.3 校对结论、冲突与使用规则

1.  `small/medium/x-medium/large/x-large/2x-large/3x-large/4x-large/5x-large/pill`
    的名称语义和解析数值可逐项对齐，共 10 条
    `resolved-value-match`；相同名称/值仍不证明 Figma Variable binding。
2.  `circle` 与 `pill` 的 Figma
    语义明确不同：`circle=50%H`，`pill=999px`。本地两者却都解析为
    999，虽然 Variable ID 不同，仍不能把 `circle` 的 999 当作 `50%H`
    的无损同义值。
3.  `radius-circle` 在解除 blocker 前不得用于实现。Design System owner
    必须选择并版本化一种裁决：将 circle 改为可表达百分比/高度关联的
    Token；批准 `circle identity → 50%` 的确定性转换并强制
    `width=height`；或修改 Figma 规范为 999px 并证明所有 circle consumer
    均保持正方形。任何方案都须保留旧/新 path、Variable
    ID、consumer、迁移与 baseline。
4.  Figma 圆形示例是 80×80、实际 radius=40。`50%` 只有在 `width=height`
    时产生正圆；非正方形使用会成为椭圆，因此 circle 合同必须同时验证
    shape invariant。`pill` 则面向长条形状，禁止与 circle 互换。
5.  Figma 已明确固定项单位为 px，但 `radius.json` number
    没有单位。source-map 必须记录从 Token number 到 Web px
    的批准转换；禁止把相同 number 用到非 radius 属性，也不能将
    `ALL_SCOPES` 解释为任意用途许可。
6.  `small`、`medium`、`large`、`x-large` 的 `$description="边框宽度"`
    与文件类型、scope 及 Figma 圆角表冲突，是 4 项 metadata
    defect。它们不改变当前数值/身份，但必须通过版本化描述修复，不得复制错误描述到生成文档。
7.  `4x-large` scope=`ALL_SCOPES` 比其他 Token
    更宽；消费审计仍只能允许其用于经本规范和页面 provenance 证明的
    radius 属性，不能因 scope 宽泛而绕过语义。
8.  Figma 对 `small`、`medium` 的应用场景原文为 `/`，应记录为
    `figma-not-defined`，不能从尺寸、相邻行或行业惯例补用途。
9.  两份来源都没有 0 圆角 Token/条目，确认不能用最小 4 替代
    0，也不能伪造 `radius-none`。
10. 升级为 `bound` 需要：固定真实 immutable version；保存 root 与四列
    raw/hash；取得各行 Variable refs；逐项对账本地 Variable ID；处理四项
    description defect；关闭 circle conflict 并验证 shape
    invariant；mapping unresolved/conflict=0。否则最高保持当前
    pending/block 状态。

#### 3.8.10 Token audit 与发布条件

每个 acceptance release 的 `sources/tokens/token-audit.json`
至少必须包含：七文件相对路径、精确 mode、实际 SHA-256、Token/Variable ID
计数与唯一性、alias edge/chain/cycle/unresolved、跨层与反向依赖、direct
value 清单、命名/值疑点台账、Light/Dark 覆盖、34
个组件命名空间及非法越界消费、Sidebar selected-icon component Token →
semantic `fg-color/black-strong` 的真实 alias chain、Typography
未决映射、Spacing/Radius scope 违规、Figma Spacing 12 条 mapping 与
`rem×16=px` 校验、9 条 exact hook join、3 个 naming conflict、本地 24px
coverage gap、rem 输出的 root-font-size 约束、Figma Radius 11 条
mapping、`circle=50%H` 与本地 999 的冲突/shape
invariant、`circle`/`pill` 身份保持，以及 `Avatar.border-color.Color`
不存在断言。

同一 release 还必须保存 `sources/tokens/runtime/` 下实际加载的
package/version、导入入口、编译 artifact bytes/hash、selector/block
清单与 source→CSS lowering 报告。报告必须分开统计 global
declaration、CSS `var()` alias、literal declaration、各 theme
declaration、source path missing、runtime extra、resolved-value mismatch
与非法 consumer；不得把 CSS alias 数量冒充 Figma alias
edge，也不得从编译名反推 Variable ID。当前观测基线是
`lingee-ui@0.0.7`、artifact
SHA-256=`1053f5de446a82ed477d077d4a8c47e3ce60f5b1ddec3544d1b0debcd27bf8ef`、global=`466`、CSS
aliases=`47`、literals=`419`、Dark=`156`、Dark source
missing=`50`、resolved mismatch=`25`，它们是待关闭的 checkout 事实，不是
release pass 阈值。

只有 canonical 七源均 materialize 且 mode/hash/identity
可证，unresolved/cycle/非法消费为 0，所有 direct value/命名迁移/coverage
gap 有 disposition，Sidebar alias 通过，scope
与单位转换检查通过，三个复合规范 mapping 满足各自关闭条件，七文件 hash
与 Manifest/Evidence Pack 一致，并且目标 runtime lowering 的 source
missing/runtime extra/resolved mismatch/非法 consumer 全为 0 时，Token
合同才可从 `registered_alias_audit_pending` 升为
`audited`；这仍不单独推导页面 `verified`。

### 3.9 Lingee UI 官方图标库合同

本节覆盖并废止 v2.4.0 及更早版本中"从 Figma 基础图标 Section 读取、导出
SVG、以 component ID/key 作为运行时身份"的全部规则。自 v2.5.0 起，Figma
页面实例仍可提供**图标语义、位置、尺寸、状态和颜色 Token
provenance**，但产品图标的可执行几何、组件 API 和运行时身份只能来自官方
Lingee UI 图标库。

#### 3.9.1 当前运行时 authority 与历史审计快照

---

字段 固定合同

---

人工选图入口 [Lingee UI / Foundation /
Icon](https://kingdee.github.io/lingee-ui/#/foundation/icon)，仅用于人工导航/旁证

当前机器读取与运行时包 目标构建 lockfile + installed package 的 `lingee-icon@1.0.16`
bytes、types、exports/API

当前 authority `target-lockfile-plus-installed-package-bytes-types-and-api`；online
docs/部署站点不得覆盖

当前 evidence paths `sources/lingee-icon/1.0.16/`、`candidate-intakes/`、compatibility map、used-icons
report

当前 integrity 状态 npm integrity、tarball/catalog/type-index/sprite SHA-256 与完整 inventory 尚未
materialized；`block`

历史审计快照 `lingee-icon@1.0.15` 的 integrity/hash 与 27 类/966 exports、967 catalog rows、965
exact matches

历史快照规则 只作历史差异线索；任何 hash、计数、目录缺陷或 export 结论不得重标为 1.0.16

常用尺寸 16 / 18 / 20 / 24px；仅作候选，最终值仍由页面/组件合同决定

合同状态 `current-runtime-1.0.16-resolved_integrity-artifacts-and-page-icon-mapping-blocked`
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

网站页面只用于人类浏览与旁证，不能构造或覆盖目标构建图标。机器必须从目标
lockfile 与 installed `lingee-icon@1.0.16` 读取 package metadata、types
和实际 exports；正式 G-IC 还必须落盘 1.0.16
integrity/hash/catalog/sprite/compatibility/used
report。生产构建不得使用 `latest`、浮动 semver、未锁定 CDN、Figma 临时
URL，亦不得借用 1.0.15 历史 hash 关闭 1.0.16 缺口。

#### 3.9.2 机器读取顺序

每个页面图标必须按以下顺序解析；任一步失败都输出 blocker，不得继续猜测：

1.  从页面实例或组件合同取得 `logical_name`、业务语义、尺寸、状态、颜色
    Token、可访问性语义，以及可选的 Figma instance node。
2.  从目标 lockfile/installed `lingee-icon@1.0.16`
    的目录（若存在）按语义选择候选；目录只作候选，最终必须落到大小写完全一致的
    PascalCase runtime `export_name`。禁止用 1.0.15
    历史目录声明计数、视觉相似、中文翻译或子串命中自动裁决。
3.  在 installed `dist/index.d.ts` 与实际 package export 中双重确认该
    `export_name` 存在；二者不一致时
    `ICON_PACKAGE_INDEX_MISMATCH=block`。1.0.15 的 `GitFork` /
    `ExclamationCircleFill（详情）` 差异仅是 historical defect
    ledger，除非 1.0.16 重新审计确认，否则不得直接套用或自动去括号。
4.  将裁决写入版本化 `icon-compatibility-map.json`，保存
    owner、原因、来源页面、批准记录和适用状态；禁止运行时模糊查找。
5.  React 生成器输出静态 named import；纯 HTML
    生成器使用相同固定包版本的本地 sprite ID。生成后校验 DOM
    `data-icon`/`<use href>`、尺寸、computed color 与 Manifest 一致。
6.  页面图标的截图 baseline、交互状态与 a11y 通过后，该映射才可进入
    `verified`；仅能成功 import 不等于视觉已验证。

#### 3.9.3 稳定身份与 Page Manifest 映射

图标稳定身份固定为：

```text
package_name + exact_package_version + export_name
```

`logical_name` 只用于页面语义，Figma component/node/key
只作为可选映射证据，均不能替代
`export_name`。每个实际使用图标必须有如下记录：

```yaml
icon_usage:
  logical_name: "sidebar-search"
  source:
    docs_url: "https://kingdee.github.io/lingee-ui/#/foundation/icon"
    package_name: "lingee-icon"
    package_version: "1.0.16"
    export_name: "Search"
    category: "通用类"
  page_evidence:
    figma_instance_node_id: "OPTIONAL_WHEN_NOT_PRESENT_IN_FIGMA"
    figma_component_node_id: "OPTIONAL_VISUAL_MAPPING_ONLY"
    semantic_reason: "搜索"
  render:
    size: 20
    color_token: "REQUIRED_COMPONENT_OR_SEMANTIC_TOKEN_PATH"
    fill: "currentColor"
    rotate: 0
    spin: false
  accessibility:
    decorative: true
    accessible_name: null
  source_map_entry: "REQUIRED"
  mapping_status: "approved|blocked"
```

同一 `logical_name` 在不同状态使用不同 export 时必须显式登记 state
mapping；同一个 export
可被多个语义消费，但每个消费者都须独立记录。`Search` 与
`SearchAnalysis`、`Play` 与 `Play1`、带 `1/2` 后缀的 export
都是不同身份，禁止静默合并。

#### 3.9.4 React 与纯 HTML 使用合同

React/React 兼容项目必须使用 tree-shakable named import：

```tsx
import { Search } from "lingee-icon";

<Search size={20} color="currentColor" aria-hidden="true" />;
```

禁止复制包内 SVG path 到业务仓库、通过字符串动态索引未知组件、读取
`node_modules` 私有实现路径，或把官方组件转存成未经版本约束的散落 SVG。

纯 HTML 项目必须把固定版本 `dist/svg-sprite.js` 纳入受 hash
约束的发布包并只加载一次，然后使用精确 ID：

```html
<svg width="20" height="20" fill="currentColor" aria-hidden="true">
  <use href="#Search"></use>
</svg>
```

禁止生产环境引用
`https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/lingee-icon@latest/...`。若必须走
CDN，也只能使用精确版本并同时校验 SRI、离线 fallback 与最终字节
hash；默认策略仍是随 release bundle 本地化。

#### 3.9.5 尺寸、颜色、状态与可访问性

1.  `size` 同时设置宽高；必须来自 Figma 页面实例或组件合同。16/18/20/24
    只是官方常用值，不能把非标准设计尺寸强制归一。
2.  默认使用 `color="currentColor"`，由组件 Token 或允许的语义 Token
    控制。不得把页面 observed HEX、包内默认 paint 或 Figma
    示例色硬编码进业务组件。
3.  `fill` 仅在设计明确要求多色/渐变且固定包实现支持时使用；不得修改官方
    path。`rotate`、`spin` 只能由明确状态/动效合同开启。
4.  default/hover/pressed/selected/active/disabled/focus-visible 的
    export、size、color、opacity 与 motion 必须按 legal tuple
    记录。未登记 compound state fail closed。
5.  装饰图标必须 `aria-hidden=true`；独立图标按钮必须由按钮提供稳定
    accessible name，不能把 export 名直接暴露为用户文案。
6.  验收至少断言 exact package version、exact export、DOM `data-icon` 或
    sprite ID、rendered width/height、computed
    color、焦点/禁用状态及截图 baseline。

#### 3.9.6 缺失图标、禁用来源与新增流程

当 `ICONS.md`、类型索引或实际 export 中不存在所需图标时：

- 记录 `ICON_NOT_FOUND`，包含 logical name、业务语义、页面/Figma
  evidence、候选被拒原因、owner 和解除条件。
- 停止该图标及依赖它的验收范围；不得自动选相似图标。
- 由 Design System owner 向 `lingee-icon`
  官方库新增图标并发布新固定版本，再更新本节版本/integrity/hash、compatibility
  map、lockfile 与全部受影响 baseline。
- 禁止 `@ant-design/icons`、`lucide-react`、其他第三方图标包、Figma
  导出 SVG、业务仓库散落 SVG、emoji、Unicode glyph、CSS
  手绘、截图和模糊位图作为 fallback。

#### 3.9.7 Evidence Pack 与升级条件

每个 acceptance release 必须保存
`sources/lingee-icon/<exact-version>/`，至少包括 package
metadata、lockfile slice、npm integrity、tarball
SHA-256、`ICONS.md`、`dist/index.d.ts`、导出清单、sprite（若适用）、各文件
hash、`icon-compatibility-map.json`、used-icons report 与
license。`used-icons report` 必须证明：

- 所有 Manifest icon 都存在于固定 package
  export；missing/unknown/fuzzy-match=0。
- 每个 `logical_name × state` 都有精确
  `export_name`、size、Token、a11y 和 source-map。
- 构建产物只包含允许的 `lingee-icon` 来源，不含禁止包、散落 SVG 或临时
  Figma URL。
- package version、lockfile、运行时模块、类型索引、目录、sprite 与
  Evidence Pack hash 一致。
- 所有目标 viewport/theme/state 的 geometry、computed
  color、交互与截图 baseline 通过。

只有上述条件全部满足，且页面/Figma 语义映射 unresolved=0，图标合同才可从
`catalogued` 推进到 `implementation_ready`/`verified`。升级
`lingee-icon` 版本必须作为设计系统迁移：重新生成目录与 export
diff、复核全部 mapping、重跑视觉矩阵；禁止无审计跟随最新版本。

#### 3.9.8 附件图标 current-live candidate intake

Figma file `c6v3V45f3zltmKeSNBgR1f` 的 current-live Section
`附件-中尺寸`（root
`9839:3192`，`1054×301`）已登记为**受阻候选证据**，机器记录固定在
`sources/lingee-icon/1.0.16/candidate-intakes/attachment-medium.current-live.json`。该记录不是
active `icon-compatibility-map.json`，不能关闭 G-IC，也不改变 §3.9.1
的运行时 authority。

---

Figma node raw 32px runtime export 状态
name candidate

---

`10831:1916` CSV `FiletypeCsv` `candidate-unverified`

`25219:19897` YAML `FiletypeYml` `candidate-owner-review-required`

`10831:1915` VID --- `blocked-icon-not-found`

`10831:1911` IMG `FiletypeImg` `candidate-unverified`

`10831:1910` PPT `FiletypePpt` `candidate-duplicate-source-review-required`

`10831:1912` XLS `FiletypeXls` `candidate-unverified`

`10831:1909` RAR `FiletypeRar` `candidate-unverified`

`10831:1908` 通用 --- `blocked-semantic-owner-decision-required`

`10831:1907` ZIP `FiletypeZip` `candidate-unverified`

`10831:1906` PPT `FiletypePpt` `candidate-duplicate-source-review-required`

`10831:1905` DOC `FiletypeDoc` `candidate-unverified`

`10831:1914` JOSN `FiletypeJson` `candidate-owner-review-required`

`10831:1904` WORD `FiletypeWord` `candidate-unverified`

`10831:1903` TXT `FiletypeTxt` `candidate-unverified`

`10831:1901` JS `FiletypeJs` `candidate-unverified`

`10831:1900` PY `FiletypePy` `candidate-unverified`

`25219:19916` MCP --- `blocked-icon-not-found`

`10831:1899` HTML `FiletypeHtml` `candidate-unverified`

`10831:1902` MD `FiletypeMd` `candidate-unverified`
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

计数固定为 19 个 source nodes、16 个有 export 候选的 nodes、15 个
distinct candidate exports、3 个 unresolved nodes、0 个 approved
mappings。两个 PPT node 必须保持独立 source
identity；`YAML → FiletypeYml` 与 Figma 原始拼写 `JOSN → FiletypeJson`
必须经 Design System owner 明确复核，禁止静默规范化。VID、通用、MCP
不得自动替换为近似 export。

当前 blocker 至少包括
`FIGMA_IMMUTABLE_VERSION_MISSING`、`FIGMA_COMPONENT_IDENTITY_UNRESOLVED`、`FIGMA_SNAPSHOT_HASH_MISSING`、`VISUAL_MAPPING_UNVERIFIED`、`LINGEE_ICON_1_0_16_INTEGRITY_ARTIFACTS_NOT_MATERIALIZED`、`CODE_REVISION_UNAVAILABLE`
与三个 unresolved export。唯一允许声明是
`current_live_icon_candidate_registered`；禁止声明
`official_icon_package_updated`、`approved_icon_mapping`、`figma_source_exact`、`visual_threshold_verified`
或 `production_ready`。

Figma 在此只提供 current-live 语义与视觉候选证据，不提供运行时
bytes。实现不得保存 Figma 临时 asset URL，不得导入 Figma SVG，不得修改
`node_modules`；后续只能在取得 immutable version、component
identity、持久化 hash/baseline、1.0.16 完整 Evidence Pack 与 owner
批准后，将已裁决条目迁入正式 compatibility map。

# 第二层：Archetype Contract

## 4. 页面原型合同

### 4.1 Archetype 的职责

Archetype
只定义结构槽位、组合约束和通用状态类别，不承载业务文案、业务数据或未经证明的视觉值。每份
Archetype Contract 至少必须包含：

- `archetype_id`、版本、适用 platform/viewport/shell。
- 来源 Figma 的 `file_key`、`file_url`、真实
  `immutable_version_id`、`canonical_version_request`，以及至少一个
  `source_page_root_url`；若结构由局部 FRAME 提炼，还必须逐项登记
  `source_structure_root_urls[]`，每条 URL 同时保存 node ID、预期
  type/raw name 与 snapshot SHA-256。
- 必需、可选、互斥 slot；每个 slot 允许的 component contract。
- DOM landmark 和阅读/焦点顺序。
- 内容流、滚动、溢出、sticky/overlay 边界。
- critical states 与五类异常态的容器归属。
- 可继承和必须由 Page Manifest 实例化的字段。
- 明确的 `must_not_infer` 列表。
- `composition_authority=validator-only`、`slot_auto_instantiation_allowed=false`
  与 `content_defaults_allowed=false`；Archetype
  只能判定页面实例是否合法，不能为页面补实例。
- 结构、交互、响应式和 a11y 的证据链接。

页面级 node 与 Archetype
冲突时停止并升级合同；不得用"原型通常如此"覆盖页面证据。

Archetype 的最小 Figma 来源记录固定如下；任何 active Archetype
缺少这些一一对应直链时，`G-FL=block`：

```yaml
archetype_contract_schema: "2.3.0"
schema_ref: "schemas/archetype-contract-2.3.0.schema.json"
composition_authority: "validator-only"
slot_auto_instantiation_allowed: false
content_defaults_allowed: false
figma_source:
  file_key: "REQUIRED"
  file_url: "REQUIRED_CLICKABLE_FIGMA_FILE_URL"
  immutable_version_id: "REQUIRED_REAL_FIGMA_VERSION_ID"
  canonical_version_request: "REQUIRED_EXACT_VERSIONED_REQUEST_WITHOUT_SECRET"
  source_page_root:
    node_id: "REQUIRED"
    url: "REQUIRED_CLICKABLE_EXACT_NODE_URL"
    expected_type: "FRAME|COMPONENT|SECTION"
    expected_raw_name: "REQUIRED_EXACT_RAW_NAME"
  source_structure_roots:
    - node_id: "REQUIRED_OR_EMPTY_ONLY_WHEN_PAGE_ROOT_IS_COMPLETE_SOURCE"
      url: "REQUIRED_CLICKABLE_EXACT_NODE_URL"
      expected_type: "FRAME|COMPONENT|COMPONENT_SET|SECTION"
      expected_raw_name: "REQUIRED_EXACT_RAW_NAME"
  snapshot_sha256: "REQUIRED_REAL_SHA256"
```

### 4.2 当前 archetype 登记（active 空；pending intake=1）

---

登记项 当前值 规则

---

pending 1（`workbench-home`） 仅为待 immutable-version 锁版的 live
typical evidence，不计 archetype source page
page intake

active 0 当前不存在可继承的页面原型合同
archetype

archetype 0 pending intake 尚未在真实 immutable
source version 上完成直读与 Page
pages Manifest；当前没有可填入
`source_page_root.url` 的 active
来源链接

candidate `list`、`table`、`form`、`dashboard`、`chat`、`settings`、`auth`、`error` 全部仅为 `not_registered` 候选，不构成
types identity、结构或生成许可
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

新 archetype 只能在至少一个新 page node 已按真实 immutable version
完整直读、形成通过 schema 校验的 Page Manifest 与 Evidence Pack
后建立。`workbench-home` 的 live identity、用户确认 matrix 与
current-live child geometry 均不足以建立
archetype；候选名称、历史页面名称、现有 route/DOM/CSS
或组件组合也不得自动生成 archetype，名称相同不得建立历史关联。

### 4.3 Archetype 继承规则

1.  Manifest 必须显式写 archetype 版本；禁止隐式使用"最新"。
2.  Archetype 的 required slot 不能被页面删除，但 required
    只是一条校验约束：Page Manifest 必须引用典型页面中真实存在且具有
    exact lineage 的填充实例；缺失时 `block`，Archetype
    和生成器都不得自动填充。可选 slot 只有典型页面 exact
    instance/property 明确开启时才生成，不能仅因 Archetype 允许而出现。
3.  视觉验收的业务内容只能来自 deterministic approved
    fixture；生产运行仅在 Manifest 选择 `production_data_contract`
    时，按已批准 API/data contract 注入。Archetype、Figma 或 fixture
    的示例文案/数据不得成为生产默认值。
4.  archetype 响应式规则必须有对应 viewport node/baseline 或批准的工程
    fallback；否则为 P0 gap。
5.  一个页面可引用多个已批准局部 pattern，但只能有一个主 archetype；每个
    pattern 仍必须由典型页面中的 exact instance/root 明确授权，Archetype
    不得主动插入。Page Manifest 只能在 Archetype
    明确开放的字段中实例化或收紧；与硬约束冲突时必须停止，并通过版本化
    `exception_contract` 或升级 Archetype 解决，禁止因
    Manifest"更具体"而静默覆盖。
6.  Archetype 的 `must_not_infer` 至少包含：未实例化
    Slot、默认卡片/按钮/筛选器、标题/说明/placeholder、导航项、空态/推荐内容、额外交互、断点重排和相似组件替换。某项未来要成为共享规则，必须先由新的
    versioned typical-page evidence 建立并审批，不能由实现反推。

# 第三层：Page Manifest

### 5.0 HTML conversion manifest 边界

`html-source-generation` 不属于本层的 Figma Page Manifest delivery modes。
其唯一机器清单是 HSI-4 的 `html_conversion_manifest`；该清单记录 HTML 四层身份、
安全解析、依赖闭包、Lingee mapping 与生成工程证据，不得出现或推导
`source.figma`、active archetype、composition exact、G-PC 或 formal release readiness。

## 5. Page Manifest 完整 YAML 模板

每个正式 Figma-backed 页面必须单独落一份符合以下 schema 的
YAML。`REQUIRED`、`unknown`、空 evidence 或未关闭 gap 都不能通过
Readiness Gate。`repository-reference-generation` 禁止写入
`generation_mode`，也禁止为 §5.4 profile 伪造以下 Figma 必填字段；它使用
§0.3.3/§7.1.3 的独立报告。`html-source-generation` 同样不得复用本模板；
它必须使用 HSI-4 的独立 `html_conversion_manifest`，否则会伪造不存在的
Figma file/node/version、active Page Manifest 与 G-PC 状态。

```yaml
page_manifest_schema: "2.3.0"
schema_ref: "schemas/page-manifest-2.3.0.schema.json"
release_id: "REQUIRED_STABLE_RELEASE_ID"
page_id: "REQUIRED_STABLE_PAGE_ID"
manifest_version: "REQUIRED_SEMVER"
generation_mode: "exact-state-preview|interactive-prototype|production"
contract_status: "catalogued|extracted|contract_complete|implementation_ready|implemented_unverified|verified|blocked"
title: "REQUIRED"
business_content_policy: "approved_fixture_only|production_data_contract" # REQUIRED_ENUM；必须二选一
provenance_required: true
renderer:
  renderer_profile_id: "REQUIRED"
  renderer_profile_version: "REQUIRED_PINNED_VERSION"
  renderer_profile_path: "REQUIRED_REPOSITORY_RELATIVE_PATH"
  renderer_profile_sha256: "REQUIRED_REAL_SHA256"
readiness:
  derived_only: true
  generation_ready: false
  figma_link_validation_pass: false
  page_composition_closure_pass: false
  target_matrix_cell_count: "REQUIRED_POSITIVE_INTEGER"
  composition_plan_cell_count: "REQUIRED_SAME_POSITIVE_INTEGER"
  blocking_reason_codes: ["REQUIRED_OR_EMPTY_AFTER_VALIDATION"]
owners:
  product: "REQUIRED"
  design: "REQUIRED"
  engineering: "REQUIRED"
  qa: "REQUIRED"
source:
  figma:
    file_key: "REQUIRED"
    file_url: "REQUIRED_CLICKABLE_FIGMA_FILE_URL"
    canvas_node_id: "REQUIRED"
    page_node_id: "REQUIRED_ROOT_PAGE_NODE"
    page_node_url: "REQUIRED_CLICKABLE_EXACT_PAGE_NODE_URL"
    page_node_role: "page_instance_root"
    expected_page_node_type: "FRAME|COMPONENT|SECTION"
    expected_page_raw_name: "REQUIRED_EXACT_RAW_NAME"
    immutable_version_id: "REQUIRED_REAL_FIGMA_VERSION_ID"
    canonical_version_request: "REQUIRED_EXACT_VERSIONED_REQUEST_WITHOUT_SECRET"
    version_url: "OPTIONAL_HUMAN_LOCATOR"
    version_created_at: "REQUIRED_ISO8601"
    version_last_modified: "REQUIRED_ISO8601"
    captured_at: "REQUIRED_ISO8601"
  icon_library:
    documentation_url: "https://kingdee.github.io/lingee-ui/#/foundation/icon"
    package_name: "lingee-icon"
    package_version: "1.0.16"
    runtime_authority: "target-lockfile-plus-installed-package-bytes-types-and-api"
    npm_integrity: "REQUIRED_NOT_MATERIALIZED"
    evidence_path: "sources/lingee-icon/1.0.16/"
    historical_audited_snapshot_ref: "front-matter.icon_library_spec_source.historical_audited_package_version"
    validation_status: "blocked_pending_1.0.16_integrity_artifacts"
  audit_sources: []
source_ingestion:
  mode: "direct_figma"
  tool:
    name: "REQUIRED"
    version: "REQUIRED_PINNED_VERSION"
    connector: "REQUIRED"
    connector_version: "REQUIRED_PINNED_VERSION"
  read_scope:
    root_node_id: "REQUIRED"
    recursive_descendants: true
    include_hidden_layers: true
    skipInvisibleInstanceChildren: false
    include_instance_internals: true
    resolve_reference_closure: true
    viewports: ["REQUIRED"]
    themes: ["REQUIRED"]
    states: ["REQUIRED"]
  version_probe:
    before_path: "REQUIRED"
    after_path: "REQUIRED"
    unchanged: true
  source_version_capability:
    path: "REQUIRED_source-version-capability.json"
    sha256: "REQUIRED_REAL_SHA256"
    source_version_coherence: "pass"
  snapshot:
    metadata_path: "REQUIRED"
    nodes_path: "REQUIRED_NDJSON"
    components_path: "REQUIRED"
    variables_path: "REQUIRED"
    styles_path: "REQUIRED"
    assets_path: "REQUIRED"
    fonts_path: "REQUIRED"
    interactions_path: "REQUIRED"
    hashes_path: "REQUIRED"
    canonical_snapshot_sha256: "REQUIRED_REAL_SHA256"
  completeness_report:
    path: "REQUIRED"
    sha256: "REQUIRED_REAL_SHA256"
    result: "pass"
    direct_read_complete: true
  source_map:
    path: "REQUIRED"
    sha256: "REQUIRED_REAL_SHA256"
    unmapped_value_count: 0
  pagination:
    page_count: "REQUIRED_INTEGER"
    chunk_count: "REQUIRED_INTEGER"
    all_chunks_reconciled: true
  truncation: false
figma_feature_support:
  - feature: "REQUIRED_FEATURE_CLASS"
    read_support: "supported|partial|unsupported|not_present"
    generation_support: "supported|partial|unsupported|not_applicable"
    verification_support: "supported|partial|unsupported|not_applicable"
    evidence: "REQUIRED"
    impact: "none|block"
unsupported_features: []
manual_overrides: [] # 非空项必须含 owner/reason/evidence/expiry/approval/locator/before/after，且 source_exact=false
fidelity:
  derived_only: true
  direct_read_complete: false
  source_exact: false
  composition_exact: false
  structure_exact: false
  behavior_verified: false
  visual_threshold_verified: false
  literal_pixel_exact: false
classification:
  archetype_id: "REQUIRED_APPROVED_OR_EXPLICIT_NONE"
  archetype_version: "REQUIRED_PINNED_SEMVER_OR_NOT_APPLICABLE"
  platform: "web|client"
  shell_id: "REQUIRED_OR_NONE"
  route_kind: "public|authenticated|internal|not_applicable"
target:
  repository_root: "REQUIRED"
  route: "REQUIRED_OR_NOT_APPLICABLE"
  stack_detection:
    project_state: "non_empty|empty"
    detected_stack: ["REQUIRED"]
    evidence_files: ["REQUIRED"]
    preserve_existing_stack: true
  implementation:
    entrypoints: ["REQUIRED"]
    allowed_paths: ["REQUIRED"]
    forbidden_paths: ["REQUIRED_OR_EMPTY"]
  dependencies:
    additions_allowed: false
    approvals: []
render_matrix:
  viewports:
    - id: "REQUIRED_VIEWPORT_ID"
      width: "REQUIRED_INTEGER"
      height: "REQUIRED_INTEGER"
      device_scale_factor: "REQUIRED_NUMBER"
      figma_node_id: "REQUIRED"
      figma_node_url: "REQUIRED_CLICKABLE_EXACT_VIEWPORT_NODE_URL"
      evidence_status: "pass|partial|block"
  themes:
    - id: "light|dark"
      requested_theme: "light|dark|null"
      resolved_theme: "light|dark"
      authority: "user-explicit|figma-target"
      authority_locator: "REQUIRED"
      support_status: supported
      color_scheme: "REQUIRED_EQUAL_RESOLVED_THEME"
      token_mode: "lingee-ui-root-light|lingee-ui-dark-override"
      token_artifact_path: "REQUIRED"
      token_artifact_sha256: "REQUIRED_REAL_SHA256"
      html_data_theme: "REQUIRED_EQUAL_RESOLVED_THEME"
      body_data_theme: "REQUIRED_EQUAL_RESOLVED_THEME"
      portal_scope: "body|REQUIRED_NAMED_ROOT"
      page_background_token: "REQUIRED_SEMANTIC_TOKEN_PATH"
      opposite_theme_scope_count: 0
      cross_theme_semantic_value_count: 0
      # dark formal mode MUST use bg-color/page-default; approved #121214 remains blocked until source/runtime closure
  critical_states:
    - id: "REQUIRED_CRITICAL_STATE_ID"
      deterministic_fixture_id: "REQUIRED"
  baseline_cells:
    - key: "${page_id}__${viewport_id}__${theme_id}__${critical_state_id}"
      page_id: "REQUIRED"
      viewport_id: "REQUIRED_VIEWPORT_ID"
      theme_id: "REQUIRED_THEME_ID"
      critical_state_id: "REQUIRED_CRITICAL_STATE_ID"
      baseline_png: "REQUIRED"
      baseline_sha256: "REQUIRED"
      source_node_id: "REQUIRED"
      source_node_url: "REQUIRED_CLICKABLE_EXACT_STATE_NODE_URL"
      source_version_id: "REQUIRED"
      baseline_source:
        kind: "figma-versioned-render|approved-design-raster"
        request_method: "REQUIRED"
        format: png
        scale: "REQUIRED_NUMBER"
        contents_only: "REQUIRED_BOOLEAN"
        use_absolute_bounds: "REQUIRED_BOOLEAN"
        background_policy: "REQUIRED"
        expected_pixel_width: "REQUIRED_INTEGER"
        expected_pixel_height: "REQUIRED_INTEGER"
        request_fingerprint_sha256: "REQUIRED_REAL_SHA256"
      actual_capture:
        url_or_route: "REQUIRED"
        capture_state_setup: "REQUIRED"
        stability_probe: "REQUIRED"
        screenshot_path: "REQUIRED"
        screenshot_sha256: "REQUIRED_REAL_SHA256"
      status: "pass|partial|block"
composition_plan:
  schema: "1.0.0"
  schema_ref: "schemas/page-composition-1.0.0.schema.json"
  authority_mode: "exact-typical-page-first"
  cell_scoped: true
  auto_component_selection_allowed: false
  auto_slot_fill_allowed: false
  auto_content_generation_allowed: false
  similar_component_substitution_allowed: false
  cross_page_structure_or_literal_reuse_allowed: false
  cells:
    - cell_key: "${page_id}__${viewport_id}__${theme_id}__${critical_state_id}"
      source_root:
        node_id: "REQUIRED_EXACT_TYPICAL_PAGE_OR_STATE_ROOT"
        node_url: "REQUIRED_CLICKABLE_EXACT_NODE_URL"
        expected_type: "FRAME|COMPONENT|SECTION"
        expected_raw_name: "REQUIRED_EXACT_RAW_NAME"
        immutable_version_id: "REQUIRED_REAL_FIGMA_VERSION_ID"
        snapshot_path: "REQUIRED"
        snapshot_sha256: "REQUIRED_REAL_SHA256"
      source_tree:
        path: "REQUIRED_CELL_SOURCE_TREE_NDJSON"

        source_order_signature_sha256: "REQUIRED_REAL_SHA256"
      page_structure_nodes:
        - composition_node_id: "REQUIRED_STABLE_ID"
          figma_node_id: "REQUIRED"
          figma_node_url: "REQUIRED_CLICKABLE_EXACT_NODE_URL"
          parent_composition_node_id: "REQUIRED_OR_PAGE_ROOT"
          source_order_index: "REQUIRED_ZERO_BASED_INTEGER"
          node_kind: "page-root|layout-frame|layout-group|repeat-region"
          visible_or_semantic_leaf: false
          region_id: "REQUIRED"
          lowering_rule_id: "REQUIRED"
      instances:
        - instance_id: "REQUIRED_STABLE_PER_OCCURRENCE_ID"
          parent_composition_node_id: "REQUIRED_PAGE_STRUCTURE_OR_INSTANCE_ID"
          parent_definition_relative_path: "REQUIRED_OR_PAGE_ROOT"
          source_order_index: "REQUIRED_ZERO_BASED_INTEGER"
          instance_kind: "composite|atomic|page-local-component"
          figma_instance_node_id: "REQUIRED"
          figma_instance_node_url: "REQUIRED_CLICKABLE_EXACT_INSTANCE_NODE_URL"
          source_presence: "exact-page-instance|exact-composite-definition-child"
          component_key: "REQUIRED_REAL_KEY"
          contract_id: "REQUIRED"
          contract_version: "REQUIRED_PINNED_VERSION"
          definition_root_node_id: "REQUIRED"
          definition_root_node_url: "REQUIRED_CLICKABLE_EXACT_DEFINITION_ROOT_URL"
          exact_variant_node_id: "REQUIRED"
          exact_variant_node_url: "REQUIRED_CLICKABLE_EXACT_VARIANT_URL"
          tuple_id: "REQUIRED_LEGAL_TUPLE_ID"
          slot_owner_instance_id: "REQUIRED_OR_NONE"
          slot_path: "REQUIRED_OR_NONE"
          visibility_source: "REQUIRED_EXACT_NODE_OR_PROPERTY_EVIDENCE"
          property_values:
            - raw_name: "EXACT_PROPERTY_NAME"
              value: "REQUIRED_EXACT_VALUE"
              value_source: "page-instance-override|parent-definition-override|definition-default"
              evidence_node_id: "REQUIRED"
              evidence_property_path: "REQUIRED"
          content_binding_refs: ["REQUIRED_OR_EMPTY"]
          child_expansion_ref: "REQUIRED_COMPOSITE_RENDER_TREE_OR_NONE_FOR_ATOMIC"
      slot_resolutions:
        - owner_instance_id: "REQUIRED"
          slot_path: "REQUIRED_EXACT_SLOT_PATH"
          cardinality: "0..1|1|0..n|1..n"
          filled_instance_ids: ["REQUIRED_OR_EMPTY_ONLY_WHEN_LEGAL"]
          source_order_preserved: true
          resolution_source: "exact-page-instance|exact-parent-definition"
          result: "pass"
      content_bindings:
        - binding_id: "REQUIRED_STABLE_ID"
          owner_instance_id: "REQUIRED"
          target_property_or_slot_path: "REQUIRED"
          value_source: "figma-text-node|approved-fixture-field|production-data-contract-field"
          source_node_id: "REQUIRED_OR_NOT_APPLICABLE"
          fixture_field_ref: "REQUIRED_OR_NOT_APPLICABLE"
          production_field_ref: "REQUIRED_OR_NOT_APPLICABLE"
          exact_literal_sha256: "REQUIRED_FOR_STATIC_OR_FIXTURE_LITERAL"
          generator_authored: false
      repeat_regions: [] # 页面 source 存在 repeat 时才可非空；每项必须含 source node、item contract、fixture count/order、production bounds 与 allowed fields
      page_local_leaf_dispositions: [] # 所有生成模式必须为空；出现 leaf 时先组件化并建立 exact page-local-component contract

```

`composition_plan.cells[]` 是页面生成许可清单，不是说明性附件。每个
`components[]` 项必须与同一 cell 中恰好一个 `instances[]` 项使用同一
occurrence identity，且不得存在没有 `components[]`/render-tree
展开的已授权实例，也不得存在未被 composition plan 授权的 component
record。`layout_regions[]` 必须一一引用 exact page structure
node；`content.fields[]` 必须被至少一个 content binding
使用，未引用字段、便于演示的 filler 数据和生成器追加文案都必须判为
`UNAPPROVED_GENERATED_CONTENT_PRESENT`。同一 contract
多次出现不能去重；父子关系、Slot 路径和 source order 必须保留。

Manifest 中页面/组件设计的 `source_ingestion.mode` 固定为
`direct_figma`，图标则固定为 `source.icon_library` 所登记的 pinned
package；两者不得互相替代。`truncation` 只能在 read-report
证明所有截断均已续读并对账后写
`false`，不能由人工直接改值。`provenance_required=true` 是硬规则：每个
generated DOM node/attribute/content、CSS declaration/value、非图标
asset、component property 和 behavior transition 都必须在
`source-map.json` 中指向固定 Figma version 的
node/property/variable/style/asset，或指向经批准的产品合同；每个图标必须指向固定
`package_version + export_name` 及其页面语义/Token mapping。Page
Manifest 的视觉值只能由本次 normalized IR 与 §3.9
的已批准映射自动产生，禁止从旧规范、当前 CSS、截图或人工记录手抄。

`manual_overrides` 非空时，每项只能采用以下合同；缺一字段即 P0 blocker：

```yaml
- locator: "REQUIRED_IMPLEMENTATION_TARGET"
  before: "REQUIRED_SOURCE_VALUE"
  after: "REQUIRED_OVERRIDE_VALUE"
  owner: "REQUIRED_NAMED_OWNER"
  reason: "REQUIRED"
  evidence: "REQUIRED_STABLE_PATH_OR_URL"
  expiry: "REQUIRED_ISO8601"
  approval: "REQUIRED_APPROVAL_ID"
```

任何 override（包括已批准且未过期）都会使该声明范围
`source_exact=false`；批准只允许受控偏离，不能把偏离重命名为
source-exact 100%。`figma_feature_support` 必须覆盖本次 IR 实际出现的
feature classes；影响目标范围的 `partial/unsupported` 必须同时出现在
`unsupported_features` 和 P0 blocker 中。

`business_content_policy` 是必填二选一枚举：`approved_fixture_only`
表示该页面只消费经批准的 fixture；`production_data_contract`
表示生产运行时可按已批准且版本化的 API/data contract
注入业务数据。无论选择哪一项，**视觉验收始终必须使用 deterministic
approved fixture**，固定 locale、clock、seed
与网络输入；生产数据不得直接参与 baseline。Figma 画板、fixture
和规范中的示例业务内容只能作为验收样例，禁止把其
literal、记录或名称固化为生产默认内容；选择 `production_data_contract`
时还必须补齐 `data_contracts`、adapter、错误/重试规则及批准证据。

### 5.3 未来组合组件与典型页面投喂清单

新增来源必须先进入 pending intake，完成 immutable-version
直读、G-FL、G-DR 与 G-PC 后才可
active。仅给文件首页、Canvas、目录页、截图、组件名称或"与某页一样"均不构成生成许可。

---

投喂对象 必须提供或可由同一锁定版本完整直读得到的 exact Figma 链接/身份 缺失时处理

---

组合组件 current component set/standalone component root；每个实际允许 组合只可 `catalogued`，不得展开到页面；任何 child
variant root；每个内部 child instance 的 node lineage/Slot unknown 都
identity、component key 与 definition root；每个 Slot `COMPOSITE_INSTANCE_LINEAGE_INCOMPLETE`
owner/path；formal properties、legal tuples 与默认值来源

基础组件依赖 每个被组合或页面实际使用的 component set/standalone root；实际 只阻断受影响实例/状态，但不得用同名、近似或旧版组件替换
tuple/variant；目标  
pointer/focus/selection/availability/activity/data/structure  
state nodes；Token/style/asset/icon semantic evidence

典型页面 每个 `viewport × theme × critical_state` 的 exact page/state 不能建立 active Page Manifest 或 composition
root；页面内每个组合/组件 instance identity；page layout plan；禁止按组件库能力、Archetype 或相似页面拼装
FRAME/GROUP；实例 property overrides；Slot  
实际填充；text/asset/data-binding nodes；prototype/reaction  
evidence

重复/动态区域 repeat region root、item component/composite root、approved 禁止自动生成列表项、卡片、随机数据、placeholder 或 schema
fixture、字段 schema、确定性顺序、baseline item 外字段
count；production 还需 cardinality bounds 和 data contract

页面局部可见 leaf 应先转换为 Figma component/instance并提供 exact definition 所有生成模式均
root；若确需页面专用，建立 versioned `page-local-component` `block`；生成器不得在代码中临时组件化或直接补 HTML/CSS
exact root 与合同

Archetype（如使用） active archetype contract version、source page root 与 可显式使用 `none`/页面自包含合同；不得使用未批准候选
structure roots 的 exact links archetype，也不得让 Archetype 自动填内容

验收来源 immutable version ID/canonical versioned request、每个 cell 可学习但不可生成/验收；live URL 或 `t=` 只能作 locator
baseline source node URL、export fingerprint 与 hash
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

每次新增典型页面必须独立枚举其 literals、实例数量和 source
order。即使两个页面引用同一组合组件，也只能共享该组件的**内部合同**，不能共享页面实例、页面区域、文案、fixture、选中态或
Slot
填充。每次新增组合组件也只能扩展"可被页面引用的定义集合"，不会自动修改任何已有页面。

### 5.4 已生成典型页面内嵌 Reference Profile Registry

本节是 RRP2 `repository-reference-generation` 的唯一内容
registry，快照日期为 **2026-08-28**，registry
version=`2.0.0`。它完整登记当前仓库 5 个真实业务区域；`PagePlaceholder`
全部排除。所有 profile 使用静态 fixture、React 本地状态、无 API、无
store、无持久化；刷新后恢复 fixture 初态。公共 Shell 只定义一次，各
profile 通过 `shell_ref`
引用；该引用表示完整继承以下公共内容，不允许生成器省略 Sidebar。各
profile 与 `shared_shell` 的 `static_assets` 同时构成 repository
分支唯一 asset manifest source；本节不得旁生第二套
registry/asset/baseline 根键。


```yaml
    vite: true
    lingee_ui: "0.0.7"
    lingee_icon: "1.0.16"
    provider: "LingeeProvider(zhCN) + BrowserRouter"
    global_styles: [lingee-ui/tokens.css, lingee-ui/styles.css, src/styles/global.css]

  aliases:
    workbench-home/default-main: [工作台首页, 工作台首页页面, 新任务首页, 默认工作台]
    skill-development: [技能开发, 技能开发页面, 我的技能页面]
    agent-development: [智能体开发, 智能体开发页面]
    ceo-assistant: [CEO助理, CEO助理页面, CEO助手页面]
    key-customers: [关键客户, 关键客户页面, 风险客户页面]

  profile_defaults:
    authority:
      scope: repository-artifact-only
      design_authority: false
      page_manifest_authority: false
      archetype_authority: false
    claims:
      allowed: [repository_reference_generated, repository_reference_verified]
      forbidden: [figma_source_exact, active_page_manifest, active_archetype, production_ready]
    default_target_id: desktop-1920x1080-light
    theme_contract:
      schema: "1.0.0"
      supported_themes: [light]
      unsupported_theme_behavior: block-with-UNSUPPORTED_REFERENCE_TARGET
      document_scope: [html, body]
      portal_scope: body
      semantic_token_artifact: lingee-ui@0.0.7/tokens.css
      page_canvas_contract_ref: frontmatter.light_global_background_contract
      page_canvas_css_custom_property: --lg-g-bg-color-page-default
      page_canvas_literal_policy: forbidden
    targets:
      - {
          id: desktop-1920x1080-light,
          width: 1920,
          height: 1080,
          device_scale_factor: 1,
          theme: light,
          color_scheme: light,
          token_mode: lingee-ui-root-light,
          locale: zh-CN,
        }

  shared_shell:
    id: workbench-shell
    owners:
      component: src/pages/workbench-home/WorkbenchHomePreviewPage.tsx
      fixture: src/pages/workbench-home/workbench-home.preview-fixture.ts
      style: src/pages/workbench-home/WorkbenchHomePreviewPage.css
      route_registry: src/App.tsx
      provider: src/main.tsx
    layout:
      root: "100% × 100%; min-width 1000px; min-height 720px; overflow hidden"
      page_canvas: "inherit profile_defaults.theme_contract.page_canvas_contract_ref; no literal override"
      sidebar: "280px fixed; min-height 720px"
      header: "52px; logo 46×16px; actions 32×32px"
      navigation_row: "32px"
      management_group: "260px; child 232px"
      footer: "44px; avatar 20×20px"
    header:
      brand: { label: Lingee, logo_asset: /figma/workbench/logo.svg }
      actions:
        - { id: layout, label: 布局, icon: LayoutSidebar, behavior: no-handler }
        - { id: search, label: 搜索, icon: Search, behavior: no-handler }
        - { id: notification, label: 通知, icon: Notify, behavior: no-handler }
    global_tabs:
      default: work
      items:
        - { key: work, label: 工作, icon: List1 }
        - { key: manage, label: 管理, icon: Enterprise }
        - { key: develop, label: 开发, icon: CodeSlash }
      behavior: "切换侧栏模式；home view 同时决定右侧内嵌内容"
    work_sidebar:
      navigation:
        - { id: new-task, label: 新任务, icon: SmartQa, selected: true }
        - { id: automation, label: 自动化, icon: AlarmClock, selected: false }
        - { id: calendar, label: 日历, icon: FieldTypeDate, selected: false }
        - { id: skills, label: 技能, icon: LightningCharge, selected: false }
        - { id: agents, label: 智能体, icon: Agent, selected: false }
      navigation_behavior:
        route: unchanged
        new-task: "set selected; clear active conversation; show default-main"
        automation: "set selected; activate profiles.workbench-home/default-main.embedded_views.automation; inherit canonical new-task history"
        calendar: selection-only-default-main-unchanged
        skills: "set selected; activate profiles.workbench-home/default-main.embedded_views.skills"
        agents: "set selected; activate profiles.workbench-home/default-main.embedded_views.agents"
        dark_source_preview: "light-only embedded views [automation, skills, agents] forbidden; preserve independent dark conversation activation"
      history_tabs:
        - { key: history, label: 历史对话, selected: true }
        - { key: automation, label: 自动化任务, selected: false }
      history_source: "canonical new-task history; shared by all work navigation items; embedded views cannot override"
      history_tab_behavior: "只改变 tab selected；所有工作子页面始终显示同一 canonical conversationGroups"
      conversation_groups:
        - id: pinned
          label: 置顶
          items: [采购订单元数据配置, 供应商订单元数据]
        - id: default
          label: 默认
          items: [采购订单元数据配置, 供应商订单元数据]
        - id: cloud
          label: 云端
          projects:
            - id: cloud-supplier
              label: 供应商管理项目
              expanded: true
              items: [Q1 财务分析报告, 集团月度财务分析报告, 供应链呆滞料分析结果]
            - { id: cloud-supplier-archive, label: 供应商管理项目, expanded: false, items: [] }
        - id: local
          label: 本地
          projects:
            - id: local-group
              label: 集团管理
              expanded: true
              items: [5月考勤异常与组织效能分析, 华东区渠道合作方案优化]
            - { id: local-sales, label: 销售项目, expanded: false, items: [] }
      behavior: "项目行本地展开/收起；普通历史项 no-handler"
    develop_sidebar:
      primary_navigation:
        - { id: new-conversation, label: 新会话, icon: NewChat, selected: true }
        - { id: skill-development, label: 技能开发, icon: Plugin, selected: false }
        - { id: agent-development, label: 智能体开发, icon: Agent, selected: false }
        - { id: application-development, label: 应用开发, icon: Application, selected: false }
      secondary_navigation:
        - { id: workflow-development, label: 业务流开发, icon: Application, selected: false }
      project_header:
        label: 项目
        actions:
          - { id: new-project, label: 新建项目, icon: FolderPlus, behavior: no-handler }
          - { id: project-list, label: 项目列表, icon: Catalog2, behavior: no-handler }
      projects:
        - id: personal-management
          label: 个人管理项目
          expanded: true
          more: { label: 个人管理项目更多操作, icon: Threedots, behavior: no-handler }
          items:
            - { id: purchase-order-metadata, label: 采购订单元数据配置, indicator: running }
            - { id: sales-agent-development, label: 销售智能体开发, indicator: clarification }
            - { id: sales-script-generation, label: 销售话术技能生成, indicator: null }
            - { id: daily-news-application, label: 今日热点新闻应用, indicator: null }
        - { id: frontend-project, label: 前端工程, expanded: false, items: [] }
        - { id: backend-code, label: 后台代码集合, expanded: false, items: [] }
      behavior:
        project_rows: local-expand-collapse
        skill-development: navigate-/dev/skills
        agent-development: navigate-/dev/agents
        other_from_home: local-selection-only
        other_from-fixed-dev-route: navigate-/work/new
    manage_sidebar:
      groups:
        - id: ceo-office
          label: CEO办公室
          icon: Target
          status: 限时使用
          expanded: true
          children:
            - { id: ceo-assistant, label: CEO助理, selected: true }
            - { id: daily-summary, label: 今日摘要, selected: false }
            - { id: external-insights, label: 外部洞察专家, selected: false }
            - { id: key-customers, label: 关键客户, selected: false }
            - { id: management-analysis, label: 经营分析, selected: false }
            - { id: lingee-diary, label: 灵基日记, selected: false }
            - { id: schedule-assistant, label: 日程助理, selected: false }
        - {
            id: cfo-office,
            label: CFO办公室,
            icon: GraphUp,
            status: 限时使用,
            expanded: false,
            children: [],
          }
        - {
            id: cxo-office,
            label: CXO办公室,
            icon: Connect,
            status: 限时使用,
            expanded: false,
            children: [],
          }
        - {
            id: ai-organization,
            label: AI组织,
            icon: Organize,
            status: null,
            expanded: false,
            children: [],
          }
      conversations:
        - {
            id: management-pinned,
            label: 置顶,
            items: [今日需立即知晓的异常..., 公司当前最大经营风险],
          }
        - id: management-default
          label: 默认
          items:
            [
              近期行业变化对经营的影响,
              竞品值得关注的动态,
              今日需沉淀参考的决策,
              无需我参与的会议,
              今日公司重点关注三件事,
              最值得我投入时间的事项,
              必须我亲自处理的工作,
            ]
      behavior:
        groups: local-expand-collapse
        children: set-active-management-child
        body_switch: "CEO助理/关键客户显示专用 profile；其他 child 显示 default-main"
        conversations: no-handler
    footer:
      profile_name: 王俊
      utility_controls:
        - { label: 问题, icon: Inquire, behavior: no-handler }
        - { label: 文件入口, icon: FolderOpen, behavior: no-handler }
      dropdown:
        width: 220px
        items:
          - { label: 设置, icon: BasicSettings, behavior: no-handler }
          - { type: separator }
          - { label: 退出登录, icon: Export, danger: true, behavior: no-handler }
        behavior: local-open-close
    runtime_components:
      [
        Button,
        Divider,
        Dropdown,
        DropdownContent,
        DropdownItem,
        DropdownSeparator,
        DropdownTrigger,
        Sidebar,
        Tabs,
        Textarea,
      ]
    icons:
      [
        Agent,
        Arrow,
        AlarmClock,
        Application,
        At,
        Avatar,
        BasicSettings,
        Catalog2,
        Certificate,
        ChevronDown,
        ChevronRight,
        ChevronUp,
        CodeSlash,
        Connect,
        Display,
        Enterprise,
        Export,
        FieldTypeDate,
        FolderOpen,
        FolderPlus,
        GraphUp,
        Inquire,
        LayoutSidebar,
        LightningCharge,
        LgPlus,
        List1,
        NewChat,
        Notify,
        Organize,
        Plugin,
        ProcessOptimize,
        Search,
        SmartQa,
        Target,
        Thought,
        Threedots,
        Voice,
      ]
    static_assets: [/figma/workbench/logo.svg]

            work_navigation: automation
            header_actions:
              - { id: layout, label: 布局, icon: LayoutSidebar, behavior: no-handler }
            history_tabs: inherit-default-work
            conversation_groups: inherit-default-work
            footer_actions:
              - { id: files, label: 文件入口, icon: FolderOpen, behavior: no-handler }
              - { id: feedback, label: 问题, icon: Inquire, behavior: no-handler }
          content:
            title: 定时任务
            section_tabs:
              - { key: scheduled, label: 定时任务, selected: true }
              - { key: records, label: 执行记录, selected: false }
            search: { placeholder: 搜索技能, default_value: "" }
            create_action: { label: 新任务, icon: LgPlus }
            columns:
              - { key: name, label: 任务名称, width: 400px }
              - { key: schedule, label: 执行计划, width: 210px }
              - { key: enabled, label: 启停, width: 160px }
              - { key: status, label: 运行状态, width: 238px }
              - { key: actions, label: 操作, width: 152px }
            tasks:
              - {
                  id: daily-industry-news,
                  name: 每日行业资讯推送,
                  description: 每天上午推送AI/金融/新能源行业最新资讯和趋势摘要,
                  schedule: 间隔 60分钟,
                  enabled: true,
                  status: 下次执行 57分钟内,
                }
              - {
                  id: market-sentiment-report,
                  name: 市场舆情追踪报告,
                  description: 搜索并汇总竞争对手过去24小时在全网的舆论动态,
                  schedule: 每天 09:20,
                  enabled: true,
                  status: 下次执行 3小时内,
                }
              - {
                  id: weekly-report,
                  name: 工作周报自动生成,
                  description: 根据本周聊天、文档、表格生成简短的周报,
                  schedule: 每周五 17:55,
                  enabled: true,
                  status: 下次执行 3小时内,
                }
              - {
                  id: inventory-alert,
                  name: 库存预警检查,
                  description: 每天早上检查库存水位，识别呆滞料风险,
                  schedule: 间隔 24小时,
                  enabled: false,
                  status: 已暂停,
                  run_highlighted: true,
                }
              - {
                  id: finance-reconciliation,
                  name: 财务对账提醒,
                  description: 每月初自动核对上月财务收款与发货数据,
                  schedule: 每月1日 10:00,
                  enabled: true,
                  status: 正在执行中...,
                }
            row_menu: [编辑, 删除]
            pagination:
              total_pages: 20
              default_page: 1
              displayed_page_size: 20
              selected_page_size: 50
              options: [10, 20, 50, 100]
          default_state:
            section_tab: scheduled
            search: ""
            enabled_task_ids:
              [daily-industry-news, market-sentiment-report, weekly-report, finance-reconciliation]
            open_row_menu_task_id: daily-industry-news
            page: 1
            displayed_page_size: 20
            selected_page_size: 50
            page_size_menu_open: true
            simultaneous_overlay_fixture: [row-menu, page-size-menu]
          interactions:
            section_tabs: local-selection-content-unchanged
            search: local-filter-all-task-literals
            switches: local-toggle-refresh-restores-fixture
            run_edit_delete_create: toast-preview-feedback-no-persistence
            row_menu: local-open-close
            pagination: local-page-clamped-1-to-20
            page_size: local-select-updates-trigger-and-closes-menu
            sidebar_history: inherit-default-work-history-tabs-groups-and-local-expand-collapse
          layout:
            target: 1920×1080-light
            page_canvas: "inherit frontmatter.light_global_background_contract via --workbench-page"
            sidebar: 280×1080
            main: "1640px; content max 1160px; desktop horizontal inset 240px"
            titlebar: "52px; title 18px/28px medium"
            filters: "top 60px; 1160×40px; tabs 188×40px; search 220×36px; button 86×36px"
            table: "top 120px; 1160×348px; radius 20px; header 48px; five rows 60px"
            pagination: "top 490px; right aligned; 353×28px"
            row_menu: "128×74px; first task menu initially open"
            page_size_menu: "103×142px; initially open"
            responsive: "content width calc(100%-48px) below target; table may scroll horizontally"
          runtime_components: [Button, Dropdown, Empty, Input, Sidebar, Switch, Table, Tabs, toast]
          icons:
            [
              ChevronBarLeft,

              ChevronDown,

              ChevronRight,
              FileEarmarkPlay,
              FolderOpen,
              Inquire,
              LayoutSidebar,
              LgPlus,
              Search,
              SquareEdit,
              Threedots,
              Trash,
            ]
          static_assets: []
          fixture_integrity_notes:
            - "initial row action menu and page-size menu are simultaneously open"
            - "page-size trigger says 20 条/页 while the open menu highlights 50条/页; preserve mismatch"
            - "only automation header/footer actions are view-scoped shell overrides; history tabs/groups inherit canonical new-task content"
          claims:
            ceiling: implemented_unverified
            forbidden:
              [figma_source_exact, visual_threshold_verified_against_figma, production_ready]
      light_composer_standard:
        contract_version: "1.0.0"
        status: active-unique-repository-light-runtime-authority
        target_id: desktop-1920x1080-light
        implementation:
          class_list: [workbench-composer, workbench-composer--home-default-current-live]
          selector: ".workbench-composer.workbench-composer--home-default-current-live"
          component_owner: src/pages/workbench-home/WorkbenchHomePreviewPage.tsx
          fixture_owner: src/pages/workbench-home/workbench-home.preview-fixture.ts
          style_owner: src/pages/workbench-home/WorkbenchHomePreviewPage.css
        authority:
          scope: repository-light-runtime-only
          supersedes_prior_repository_light_composer_runtime_descriptions: true
          source_evidence_only_ref: "§3.5.2.8"
          independent_dark_contract_ref: "§3.5.2.9"
          formal_figma_provenance_status: blocked
          artifact_status: implemented_unverified
        root:
          default_desktop_rendered_size: "710×167"
          responsive_width: "calc(100% - 4px)"
          max_width: 710px
          height_behavior: auto-grow-with-input
          padding: 3px 3px 4px
          gap: 4px
          radius: 30px
          border: 0
          shadow: none
        visual_states:
          idle:
            trigger: default-and-blurred
            background_token: "var(--lg-g-bg-color-black-dim)"
            resolved_background: "#0000000a"
          focus_within:
            trigger: ":focus-within"
            source_evidence_node: "19302:20079"
            background_token: "var(--lg-g-bg-color-primary-translucent-subtle)"
            resolved_background: "#6d80ff0f"
          blurred_nonempty_draft:
            background_state: idle
            persistent_input_state: false
        surface:
          default_desktop_rendered_size: "704×124"
          width: 100%
          min_width: "min(343px, 100%)"
          max_width: 788px
          min_height: 124px
          max_height: 360px
          padding: 16px 10px 10px
          gap: 12px
          radius: 28px
          background_token: "var(--lg-g-bg-color-white-heavy)"
          resolved_background: "#ffffff"
          shadow: "0 2px 12px #00000008"
        input:
          default_desktop_rendered_size: "690×50"
          root_relative_position: "(10px, 19px)"
          min_height: 50px
          max_height: 156px
          growth: auto-height-then-overflow-y
          typography: "16px/24px; weight 400"
          background: transparent
        toolbar:
          default_desktop_rendered_size: "690×36"
          root_relative_position: "(10px, 81px)"
          gap: 20px
          leading_size: "500×36"
          trailing_size: "170×36"
          action_gap: 4px
        controls:
          add: { size: "36×36", padding: 0, icon_size: 20px }
          mention: { size: "36×36", padding: 0, icon_size: 20.333px }
          agent_picker:
            size: "104×36"
            padding: 0 8px
            label: 选择智能体
            trailing_icon: ChevronDown
            trailing_icon_size: 14px
          fast:
            size: "90×36"
            padding: 0 12px
            icon: LightningCharge
            icon_size: 16px
            trailing_icon: ChevronDown
            trailing_icon_size: 14px
          voice: { size: "36×36", padding: 0, icon_size: 20px }
          send: { size: "36×36", padding: 0, icon_size: 20px }
        context_row:
          default_desktop_rendered_size: "704×32"
          root_relative_position: "(3px, 131px)"
          padding: 0 0 0 16px
          gap: 4px
          overflow: hidden
          item_contract:
            width: auto
            min_width: 0
            height: 32px
            padding: 0 8px
            gap: 4px
            icon_size: 16px
            trailing_icon_size: 16px
          current_rendered_widths:
            local: 84px
            directory: 126px
            approval: 126px
          current_fixture:
            - { id: local, label: 本地, icon: Agent, trailing_icon: ChevronDown }
            - { id: directory, label: 选择智能体, icon: Agent, trailing_icon: ChevronDown }
            - { id: approval, label: 选择智能体, icon: Agent, trailing_icon: ChevronDown }
      content:

        mode_tabs:
          - { key: method, label: 办法, selected: true }

        composer:
```

#### 5.4.1 Registry 消费不变量

1.  `shared_shell` 是每个 profile
    的必需组成部分；生成专用内容时不得只输出右侧区域。
2.  数组顺序、重复 literals、无 handler 控件、隐藏 voice 控件和 fixture
    count mismatch
    均属于当前参考产物事实，必须原样复现；生成器不得"纠错"。
3.  只有 3 个真实 route：`/work/new`、`/dev/skills`、`/dev/agents`。CEO
    助理和关键客户必须执行 embedded activation；不得创建
    `/work/ceo/customer` 等不存在的真实 route，除非请求明确进入
    customization。
4.  所有业务状态只存内存。Agent
    删除、输入、筛选、展开和选中态刷新后恢复；禁止自动补
    API、localStorage 或状态管理库。
5.  `lingee-ui` 优先、`lingee-icon` 唯一图标源、page-local
    Card/布局边界继续遵循 §0.3.2；不得因 profile
    内嵌而退回原生高置信度控件或第三方图标。
6.  profile 内容可用于一句话生成与仓库回归；其 Figma 证据状态只读引用
    §0.3.2，不参与 formal typical-page registry 计数。
7.  所有 profile 当前只继承 `desktop-1920x1080-light`；未显式主题也必须从
    `default_target_id` 解析为 light。显式 light 必须把 html/body/color-scheme 与 Lingee root-light
    Token scope 全部闭合；显式 dark 在 dark target 未登记前返回
    `UNSUPPORTED_REFERENCE_TARGET`，禁止用 wrapper dark class、历史 dark fixture 或暗色截图绕过。

# 第四层：Evidence & Acceptance Pack

## 6. 证据包与 Readiness Gate

### 6.1 Evidence & Acceptance Pack 目录合同

必须给出映射：
